function  renderScript()  {  
    const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
    const  lines  =  rawScript.split('\n');  
    container.innerHTML  =  '';  
     
    //  Sort  markers  to  define  ranges  
    const  sortedMarkers  =  [0,  
...Array.from(beatMarkers).sort((a,
 
b)
 
=>
 
a
 
-
 
b)];
 
    renderBeatNav(sortedMarkers);  
 
    let  activeChar  =  "";  
    let  currentBeatCount  =  0;  
 

--- PAGE 101 ---
    lines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
        const  trimmed  =  line.trim();  
        if  (!trimmed  ||  isNoise(trimmed))  return;  
 
        //  Track  which  beat  we  are  currently  in  
        if  (beatMarkers.has(idx))  currentBeatCount++;  
 
        //  BEAT  ISOLATION  LOGIC  
        //  If  an  activeBeatIndex  is  set,  hide  lines  that  
aren't
 
in
 
that
 
beat
 
        if  (activeBeatIndex  !==  null  &&  currentBeatCount  !==  
activeBeatIndex)
 
return;
 
 
        //  Inject  Visual  Divider  
        if  (beatMarkers.has(idx))  {  
            const  hr  =  document.createElement('div');  
            hr.className  =  "flex  items-center  my-10  
opacity-100
 
group";
 
            hr.innerHTML  =  `  
                <div  class="flex-grow  border-t-2  
border-red-400"></div>
 

--- PAGE 102 ---
                <span  class="mx-4  text-[10px]  font-black  
text-red-500
 
tracking-[0.3em]">BEAT
 
${currentBeatCount
 
+
 
1}</span>
 
                <div  class="flex-grow  border-t-2  
border-red-400"></div>
 
            `;  
            container.appendChild(hr);  
        }  
 
        //  ...  [Existing  Logic  to  render  
Character/Dialogue/Directions]
 
...
 
        const  type  =  getLineType(line,  trimmed);  
        const  div  =  document.createElement('div');  
        div.id  =  `line-${idx}`;  
        div.className  =  `line-container  p-2  rounded-lg  
${slashMode
 
?
 
'hover:bg-red-50
 
cursor-pointer
 
border-2
 
border-transparent
 
hover:border-red-200'
 
:
 
''}`;
 
        div.onclick  =  ()  =>  handleBeatClick(idx);  
 
        //  [Inject  content  based  on  type  as  before]  
        renderLineContent(div,  type,  trimmed,  activeChar,  
idx);
 
         

--- PAGE 103 ---
        //  Update  activeChar  for  the  next  iteration  
        if  (type  ===  'character')  activeChar  =  
trimmed.replace(':',
 
'');
 
         
        container.appendChild(div);  
    });  
}  
 
function  renderBeatNav(markers)  {  
    const  nav  =  document.getElementById('beat-nav');  
    if  (markers.length  <=  1)  {  
        nav.classList.add('hidden');  
        return;  
    }  
     
    nav.classList.remove('hidden');  
    nav.innerHTML  =  `<button  onclick="isolateBeat(null)"  
class="px-4
 
py-1
 
text-[10px]
 
font-bold
 
rounded-full
 
${activeBeatIndex
 
===
 
null
 
?
 
'bg-indigo-600
 
text-white'
 
:
 
'bg-gray-200
 
text-gray-500'}">FULL
 
SCRIPT</button>`;
 
     
    markers.forEach((_,  i)  =>  {  

--- PAGE 104 ---
        const  btn  =  document.createElement('button');  
        btn.className  =  `px-4  py-1  text-[10px]  font-bold  
rounded-full
 
transition
 
${activeBeatIndex
 
===
 
i
 
?
 
'bg-red-600
 
text-white'
 
:
 
'bg-gray-200
 
text-gray-500'}`;
 
        btn.innerText  =  `BEAT  ${i  +  1}`;  
        btn.onclick  =  ()  =>  isolateBeat(i);  
        nav.appendChild(btn);  
    });  
}  
 
function  isolateBeat(index)  {  
    activeBeatIndex  =  index;  
    renderScript();  
    window.scrollTo({  top:  0,  behavior:  'smooth'  });  
}

JavaScript  
 
let  prompterTimer;  
 
//  Populate  Voices  on  load  
window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged  =  ()  =>  {  
    const  selector  =  
document.getElementById('voice-selection');
 
    const  voices  =  window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();  
    selector.innerHTML  =  voices  
        .filter(v  =>  v.lang.includes('en'))  

--- PAGE 112 ---
        .map(v  =>  `<option  
value="${v.name}">${v.name}</option>`).join('');
 
};  
 
function  speakText(text,  isPrompter  =  false)  {  
    return  new  Promise((resolve)  =>  {  
        synth.cancel();  
        const  utterance  =  new  SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);  
        const  selectedVoice  =  
document.getElementById('voice-selection').value;
 
        const  intention  =  
document.getElementById('voice-intention').value;
 
         
        utterance.voice  =  
window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().find(v
 
=>
 
v.name
 
===
 
selectedVoice);
 
 
        //  EMOTION  LOGIC:  Adjusting  TTS  parameters  
        if  (isPrompter)  {  
            utterance.volume  =  0.4;  
            utterance.pitch  =  1.2;  //  Higher  pitch  feels  more  
like
 
a
 
"whisper/hint"
 
            utterance.rate  =  0.9;  

--- PAGE 113 ---
        }  else  {  
            switch(intention)  {  
                case  'aggressive':   
                    utterance.rate  =  1.3;  utterance.pitch  =  
0.8;
 
utterance.volume
 
=
 
1.0;
 
 
                    break;  
                case  'sarcastic':   
                    utterance.rate  =  0.8;  utterance.pitch  =  
0.7;
 
 
                    break;  
                case  'vulnerable':   
                    utterance.rate  =  0.8;  utterance.pitch  =  
1.1;
 
utterance.volume
 
=
 
0.6;
 
 
                    break;  
                default:   
                    utterance.rate  =  1.0;  utterance.pitch  =  
1.0;
 
 
            }  
        }  
 
        utterance.onend  =  resolve;  
        synth.speak(utterance);  

--- PAGE 114 ---
    });  
}  
 
//  Logic  for  the  User's  Turn  
async  function  handleUserTurn(lineText)  {  
    const  useWhisper  =  
document.getElementById('whisper-toggle').checked;
 
    const  delay  =  
parseInt(document.getElementById('whisper-delay').value);
 
 
    return  new  Promise(async  (resolve)  =>  {  
        let  lineFinished  =  false;  
 
        //  Start  the  Whisper  Prompter  timer  
        if  (useWhisper)  {  
            prompterTimer  =  setTimeout(()  =>  {  
                if  (!lineFinished)  {  
                    const  hint  =  lineText.split('  ').slice(0,  
3).join('
 
');
 
//
 
First
 
3
 
words
 
                    speakText(hint  +  "...",  true);  
                }  

--- PAGE 115 ---
            },  delay);  
        }  
 
        //  Logic  for  advancing  (Tap,  Voice,  or  Timer)  
        const  triggerMode  =  
document.getElementById('voice-trigger-mode').value;
 
         
        if  (triggerMode  ===  'tap')  {  
            await  waitForClick();  
        }  else  if  (triggerMode  ===  'time')  {  
            const  waitTime  =  (lineText.split('  ').length  /  
130)
 
*
 
60000
 
+
 
2000;
 
            await  new  Promise(res  =>  setTimeout(res,  
waitTime));
 
        }  
         
        lineFinished  =  true;  
        clearTimeout(prompterTimer);  
        resolve();  
    });  
}

To  take  emotional  toning  to  the  next  level  without  a  massive  server-side  bill,  we  have  to  
move
 
beyond
 
simple
 
pitch/rate
 
shifts
 
and
 
look
 
at
 
Speech
 
Synthesis
 
Markup
 
Language
 
(SSML)
 
and
 
Prosodic
 
Variation
.
 
As  a  CTO,  I  recommend  a  "Hybrid  Persona"  approach.  Since  standard  browser  voices  
(Web
 
Speech
 
API)
 
are
 
somewhat
 
limited,
 
we
 
can
 
implement
 
Prosodic
 
Modeling
—adjusting

and

to

1.  The  Strategy:  The  "Emotional  Mapping"  Logic  
Humans  don't  just  speak  faster  when  angry;  they  use  sharper  attacks  and  shorter  
pauses.
 
We
 
will
 
build
 
a
 
Prosody
 
Map
 
that
 
overrides
 
the
 
default
 
speech
 
engine
 
based
 
on
 
the
 
actor's
 
selection.
 
Intention  Rate  Pitch  Volume  Break  (Pause)  Strategy  
Aggressive  1.4  0.8  1.0  0.1s  High  speed,  low  pitch,  "Staccato"  delivery.  
Sarcastic  0.7  0.6  0.8  0.4s  Slow,  drawling,  descending  pitch  at  end  of  lines.  
Vulnerable  0.8  1.2  0.5  0.8s  Soft,  high  pitch,  

--- PAGE 118 ---
None
frequent  long  pauses  (hesitation).  
Urgent  1.6  1.1  0.9  0.05s  Rapid  fire,  slightly  higher  pitch  for  anxiety.  
 
2.  Implementation:  The  Emotional  Tone  Engine  
We  will  update  our  speakText function  to  use  a  "Segmented  Prosody"  approach.  This  
breaks
 
the
 
text
 
into
 
smaller
 
chunks
 
to
 
vary
 
the
 
emphasis,
 
preventing
 
that
 
"Siri"
 
flatline.
 
JavaScript  
 
const  EmotionLibrary  =  {  
    aggressive:  {  rate:  1.4,  pitch:  0.7,  volume:  1.0,  pause:  
100
 
},
 
    sarcastic:   {  rate:  0.7,  pitch:  0.6,  volume:  0.8,  pause:  
400
 
},
 
    vulnerable:  {  rate:  0.8,  pitch:  1.2,  volume:  0.5,  pause:  
800
 
},
 
    urgent:      {  rate:  1.6,  pitch:  1.1,  volume:  0.9,  pause:  
50
 
},
 

--- PAGE 119 ---
    neutral:     {  rate:  1.0,  pitch:  1.0,  volume:  1.0,  pause:  
200
 
}
 
};  
 
async  function  speakWithEmotion(text)  {  
    const  intention  =  
document.getElementById('voice-intention').value;
 
    const  config  =  EmotionLibrary[intention];  
     
    //  Split  text  by  punctuation  to  inject  "Emotional  Pauses"  
    const  phrases  =  text.split(/([,.?!])/);   
     
    for  (let  phrase  of  phrases)  {  
        if  (!phrase.trim())  continue;  
         
        await  new  Promise((resolve)  =>  {  
            const  utterance  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(phrase);
 
            const  voices  =  window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();  
            utterance.voice  =  voices.find(v  =>  v.name  ===  
document.getElementById('voice-selection').value);
 
             

--- PAGE 120 ---
            //  Apply  the  Emotional  Map  
            utterance.rate  =  config.rate;  
            utterance.pitch  =  config.pitch;  
            utterance.volume  =  config.volume;  
 
            utterance.onend  =  resolve;  
            window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);  
        });  
 
        //  Inject  intentional  silence  between  phrases  based  on  
emotion
 
        if  (phrase.match(/[,.?!]/))  {  
            await  new  Promise(res  =>  setTimeout(res,  
config.pause));
 
        }  
    }  
}

CTO's  Low-Cost  Solution  for  2026:  

--- PAGE 121 ---
Instead  of  a  per-word  subscription  for  every  user,  we  implement  a  "Bake  &  Cache"  
strategy:
 
1.  The  user  selects  a  1-page  scene.  2.  The  app  sends  the  "Cue  Lines"  to  an  AI  Voice  API  once.  3.  The  app  caches  those  high-fidelity,  emotional  MP3s  locally  using  IndexedDB .  4.  The  actor  runs  the  lines  offline  using  the  high-quality  audio,  only  using  the  
browser's
 
robot
 
for
 
the
 
"Whisper
 
Prompter."
 
 
4.  Advanced  Feature:  "The  Intent  Randomizer"  
To  truly  prepare  an  actor  for  a  director's  "notes,"  we  add  a  "Surprise  Me"  button.  
●  The  Logic:  Every  time  the  scene  loops,  the  app  randomly  changes  the  reader's  
intention.
 ●  The  Benefit:  If  the  actor  can  remember  their  lines  when  the  reader  is  being  
"Sarcastic"
 
AND
 
when
 
they
 
are

aggressive  pdf  cleaner  
Lead  Developer  
Custom  Gem  

--- PAGE 122 ---
As  a  CTO,  I  know  that  "Dirty  PDFs"  are  the  #1  reason  actors  abandon  line-learning  
apps.
 
Professional
 
scripts
 
are
 
often
 
littered
 
with:
 
●  Watermarks  (e.g.,  "PROPERTY  OF  WARNER  BROS")  ●  Date  Stamps  and  Revision  Colors  (e.g.,  "Blue  Revised  -  10/12/25")  ●  Page  Headers/Footers  that  break  a  sentence  across  a  page  break.  ●  Production  Scannables  (Barcode  data  or  QR  codes).  
Our  Aggressive  PDF  Cleaner  won't  just  "strip  text";  it  will  use  Structural  Intelligence  
to
 
reconstruct
 
the
 
script.
 
 
1.  The  Strategy:  The  "Regex  +  Heuristic"  Scrub  
We  will  implement  a  multi-pass  cleaning  engine.  Instead  of  a  simple  trim(),  we  will  run  
the
 
text
 
through
 
a
 
"De-Noiser"
 
that
 
targets
 
the
 
specific
 
visual
 
patterns
 
of
 
script
 
metadata.
 
The  Cleanup  Rules:  
1.  Vertical  Noise:  Remove  lines  that  are  purely  numeric  (Page  numbers).  2.  Date/Revision  Noise:  Strip  strings  like  "10/22/26",  "Revised",  "Final  Draft",  or  
"Pink
 
Pages".
 3.  The  "Bridge"  Logic:  If  a  paragraph  ends  without  punctuation  and  the  next  line  
(after
 
a
 
page
 
break)
 
starts
 
with
 
a
 
lowercase
 
letter,
 
we
 
merge
 
them
 
into
 
a
 
single
 
dialogue
 
block.
 4.  Watermark  Scrub:  Remove  lines  that  repeat  on  every  page  (e.g.,  the  actor's  
name
 
or
 
agency).
 
 
2.  Implementation:  The  Cleaning  Engine  
We’ll  add  a  "Clean  Script"  function  that  runs  before  the  Character  Selection  screen  
appears.
 
Where  this  goes:  Update  your  processScript function  to  include  this  logic.  
JavaScript  

--- PAGE 123 ---
None
 
function  aggressiveClean(text)  {  
    let  lines  =  text.split('\n');  
     
    //  1.  Identify  "Global  Noise"  (Watermarks/Headers)  
    const  frequencyMap  =  {};  
    lines.forEach(l  =>  {  
        const  t  =  l.trim();  
        if  (t.length  >  5)  frequencyMap[t]  =  (frequencyMap[t]  
||
 
0)
 
+
 
1;
 
    });  
 
    //  2.  Filter  Pass  
    const  cleanedLines  =  lines.filter(line  =>  {  
        const  trimmed  =  line.trim();  
         
        //  Remove  if  it  appears  too  frequently  (likely  a  
watermark/header)
 
        if  (frequencyMap[trimmed]  >  5)  return  false;  
         
        //  Remove  Page  Numbers  (e.g.,  "12.",  "(12)",  "Page  
12")
 

--- PAGE 124 ---
        if  (trimmed.match(/^(\(?\d+\)?\.?|Page  \d+)$/i))  
return
 
false;
 
         
        //  Remove  Revision  markers  (e.g.,  "BLUE  REV.",  "*",  
"10/12/25")
 
        if  
(trimmed.match(/(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{2,4})|(\*)|(REV\.)/i))
 
return
 
false;
 
         
        //  Remove  purely  non-alphanumeric  noise  
(barcodes/scraps)
 
        if  (trimmed.length  >  0  &&  
!trimmed.match(/[a-zA-Z0-9]/))
 
return
 
false;
 
 
        return  true;  
    });  
 
    //  3.  Bridge  Pass  (Fixing  broken  dialogue  from  page  
breaks)
 
    let  bridgedLines  =  [];  
    for  (let  i  =  0;  i  <  cleanedLines.length;  i++)  {  
        let  current  =  cleanedLines[i];  
        let  next  =  cleanedLines[i+1];  

--- PAGE 125 ---
 
        if  (next  &&  isDialogueContinuation(current,  next))  {  
            bridgedLines.push(current.trim()  +  "  "  +  
next.trim());
 
            i++;  //  Skip  the  next  line  as  it's  now  merged  
        }  else  {  
            bridgedLines.push(current);  
        }  
    }  
 
    return  bridgedLines.join('\n');  
}  
 
function  isDialogueContinuation(curr,  next)  {  
    const  c  =  curr.trim();  
    const  n  =  next.trim();  
     
    //  If  current  line  doesn't  end  in  sentence-ending  
punctuation
 
 
    //  and  next  line  starts  with  lowercase,  it's  likely  a  
split
 
line.
 

--- PAGE 126 ---
    const  endsIncomplete  =  c.match(/[a-zA-Z0-9]$/)  &&  
!c.match(/[.?!:"]$/);
 
    const  startsLower  =  n.match(/^[a-z]/);  
     
    return  endsIncomplete  &&  startsLower;  
}

let  trainingMode  =  'standard';  //  'standard',  'ladder',  
'reverse'
 
let  currentStep  =  1;  
 
function  setProgressiveMode(mode)  {  
    trainingMode  =  (trainingMode  ===  mode)  ?  'standard'  :  
mode;
 
    currentStep  =  1;  //  Reset  progress  
     
    //  UI  Toggle  
    
document.getElementById('progressive-controls').classList.togg
le('hidden',
 
trainingMode
 
===
 
'standard');
 
    renderScript();  
}  
 
function  changeStep(delta)  {  
    currentStep  =  Math.max(1,  currentStep  +  delta);  
    renderScript();  

--- PAGE 130 ---
     
    //  Auto-scroll  to  the  bottom  of  the  visible  lines  
    const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
    container.lastElementChild?.scrollIntoView({  behavior:  
'smooth'
 
});
 
}  
 
function  renderScript()  {  
    const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
    const  lines  =  rawScript.split('\n').filter(l  =>  l.trim()  
&&
 
!isNoise(l.trim()));
 
    container.innerHTML  =  '';  
 
    //  Logic  to  calculate  which  lines  to  show  based  on  mode  
    let  visibleLines  =  lines;  
     
    if  (trainingMode  ===  'ladder')  {  
        visibleLines  =  lines.slice(0,  currentStep);  
        document.getElementById('step-counter').innerText  =  
`STEP
 
${currentStep}
 
/
 
${lines.length}`;
 

--- PAGE 131 ---
    }   
    else  if  (trainingMode  ===  'reverse')  {  
        const  start  =  Math.max(0,  lines.length  -  currentStep);  
        visibleLines  =  lines.slice(start);  
        document.getElementById('step-counter').innerText  =  
`BACKWARDS
 
${currentStep}
 
/
 
${lines.length}`;
 
    }  
 
    visibleLines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
        //  ...  Use  your  existing  rendering  logic  here  
(getLineType,
 
character
 
vs
 
dialogue)
 
...
 
        //  Ensure  that  the  "activeLine"  is  always  at  the  
bottom
 
for
 
focus
 
    });  
}

//  We  track  a  'confidence'  score  for  every  line  (0  to  100)  
let  lineConfidence  =  {};   
 
function  updateConfidence(lineIdx,  wasSuccessful)  {  
    if  (!lineConfidence[lineIdx])  lineConfidence[lineIdx]  =  0;  
     
    if  (wasSuccessful)  {  
        lineConfidence[lineIdx]  =  Math.min(100,  
lineConfidence[lineIdx]
 
+
 
20);
 
    }  else  {  
        lineConfidence[lineIdx]  =  Math.max(0,  
lineConfidence[lineIdx]
 
-
 
40);
 
    }  
    renderScript();  
}  

--- PAGE 134 ---
None
 
//  Inside  renderScript()  loop:  
const  confidence  =  lineConfidence[idx]  ||  0;  
//  Dynamic  background  color  based  on  confidence  (Green  to  Red)  
div.style.backgroundColor  =  `rgba(${255  -  (confidence  *  
2.55)},
 
${confidence
 
*
 
2.55},
 
0,
 
0.1)`;

    <script>  
        //  ---  CENTRALIZED  STATE  ---  
        let  AppState  =  {  
            rawText:  "",  
            lines:  [],  
            userChar:  "",  
            viewMode:  'full',  //  full,  mnemonic  
            trainingMode:  'standard',  //  standard,  ladder,  
reverse
 
            currentStep:  1,  
            isVoiceActive:  false,  

--- PAGE 147 ---
            isChaosActive:  false,  
            isSlashMode:  false,  
            hidePunc:  false,  
            beatMarkers:  new  Set(),  
            activeBeat:  null,  
            lineStats:  {},  //  {  index:  {peeks:  0,  score:  0}  }  
            format:  'screenplay'  
        };  
 
        //  ---  CORE  ENGINES  ---  
 
        function  processScript(isAggressive)  {  
            let  text  =  
document.getElementById('script-input').value;
 
            if(!text.trim())  return;  
 
            if(isAggressive)  text  =  aggressiveClean(text);  
            AppState.rawText  =  text;  
            detectFormat(text);  
             

--- PAGE 148 ---
            
document.getElementById('input-section').classList.add('hidden
');
 
            
document.getElementById('char-section').classList.remove('hidd
en');
 
            setupCharacterGrid();  
        }  
 
        function  aggressiveClean(text)  {  
            //  Remove  common  script  "trash"  
            return  text.split('\n')  
                .filter(l  =>  !l.match(/^(\d+\.|Page  
\d+|\d{1,2}\/\d{2}\/\d{2,4}|\*|CONT'D)/i))
 
                .filter(l  =>  l.trim().length  >  0)  
                .join('\n');  
        }  
 
        function  detectFormat(text)  {  
            const  sample  =  text.slice(0,  1000);  
            AppState.format  =  (sample.match(/[A-Z\s]+:/))  ?  
'stageplay'
 
:
 
'screenplay';
 

--- PAGE 149 ---
            document.getElementById('format-badge').innerText  
=
 
AppState.format;
 
        }  
 
        function  setupCharacterGrid()  {  
            const  chars  =  [...new  
Set(AppState.rawText.match(/^[A-Z]{2,}(?=\s|$|:)/gm))];
 
            const  grid  =  document.getElementById('char-grid');  
            grid.innerHTML  =  chars.map(c  =>  `  
                <button  onclick="selectCharacter('${c}')"  
class="px-6
 
py-3
 
bg-white
 
border-2
 
border-slate-200
 
rounded-2xl
 
font-bold
 
hover:border-indigo-600
 
hover:text-indigo-600
 
transition">
 
                    ${c}  
                </button>  
            `).join('');  
        }  
 
        function  selectCharacter(name)  {  
            AppState.userChar  =  name;  
            
document.getElementById('char-section').classList.add('hidden'
);
 

--- PAGE 150 ---
            
document.getElementById('work-section').classList.remove('hidd
en');
 
            renderScript();  
        }  
 
        //  ---  RENDER  ENGINE  (The  Heart)  ---  
 
        function  renderScript()  {  
            const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
            const  lines  =  
AppState.rawText.split('\n').filter(l
 
=>
 
l.trim());
 
            container.innerHTML  =  '';  
 
            let  currentBeat  =  0;  
            let  activeChar  =  "";  
 
            lines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
                const  trimmed  =  line.trim();  
                const  type  =  getLineType(line,  trimmed);  

--- PAGE 151 ---
                if(type  ===  'character')  activeChar  =  
trimmed.replace(':',
 
'');
 
                 
                if(AppState.beatMarkers.has(idx))  
currentBeat++;
 
 
                //  Filter  logic  for  Beats  /  Ladder  /  Reverse  
                if(!shouldShowLine(idx,  currentBeat,  
lines.length))
 
return;
 
 
                const  div  =  document.createElement('div');  
                div.id  =  `line-${idx}`;  
                div.className  =  `line-container  p-3  rounded-xl  
transition-all
 
relative
 
${AppState.isSlashMode
 
?
 
'cursor-pointer
 
hover:bg-red-50'
 
:
 
''}`;
 
                div.onclick  =  ()  =>  handleLineClick(idx);  
 
                //  Heatmap  logic  
                const  conf  =  AppState.lineStats[idx]?.score  ||  
0;
 
                if(conf  >  0)  div.style.backgroundColor  =  
`rgba(34,
 
197,
 
94,
 
${conf/200})`;
 

--- PAGE 152 ---
                if(AppState.lineStats[idx]?.peeks  >  2)  
div.style.backgroundColor
 
=
 
`rgba(239,
 
68,
 
68,
 
0.1)`;
 
 
                if(type  ===  'character')  {  
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="text-[10px]  
font-black
 
text-slate-400
 
uppercase
 
tracking-widest">${activeChar}</span>`;
 
                }  else  if(type  ===  'direction')  {  
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="text-sm  
italic
 
text-slate-400
 
opacity-60">${trimmed}</span>`;
 
                }  else  {  
                    const  isUser  =  (activeChar  ===  
AppState.userChar);
 
                    const  processed  =  processText(trimmed,  
isUser);
 
                    div.className  +=  isUser  ?  '  is-user-line  
my-1'
 
:
 
'
 
opacity-40
 
text-sm';
 
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="ghost-text  
block"
 
style="opacity:
 
${isUser
 
?
 
(document.getElementById('ghost-slider').value/100)
 
:
 
1}">${processed}</span>`;
 
                }  
 
                if(AppState.beatMarkers.has(idx))  {  

--- PAGE 153 ---
                    const  sep  =  document.createElement('div');  
                    sep.className  =  "beat-divider";  
                    sep.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="bg-slate-50  
px-4
 
text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
text-red-400
 
tracking-[0.4em]">BEAT
 
${currentBeat
 
+
 
1}</span>`;
 
                    container.appendChild(sep);  
                }  
 
                container.appendChild(div);  
            });  
            updateBeatNav();  
        }  
 
        function  shouldShowLine(idx,  currentBeat,  total)  {  
            if(AppState.activeBeat  !==  null  &&  currentBeat  !==  
AppState.activeBeat)
 
return
 
false;
 
            if(AppState.trainingMode  ===  'ladder'  &&  idx  >  
AppState.currentStep)
 
return
 
false;
 
            if(AppState.trainingMode  ===  'reverse'  &&  idx  <  
(total
 
-
 
AppState.currentStep))
 
return
 
false;
 
            return  true;  
        }  

--- PAGE 154 ---
 
        function  processText(text,  isUser)  {  
            if(!isUser)  return  text;  
            let  t  =  text;  
            if(AppState.hidePunc)  t  =  t.replace(/[^\w\s]|_/g,  
"");
 
             
            const  blankIntensity  =  
document.getElementById('blank-slider').value;
 
            if(blankIntensity  >  0)  {  
                t  =  t.split('  ').map(w  =>  Math.random()*100  <  
blankIntensity
 
?
 
'_____'
 
:
 
w).join('
 
');
 
            }  
 
            if(AppState.viewMode  ===  'mnemonic')  {  
                return  t.split('  ').map(w  =>  w[0]  ?  w[0]  +  
(w.match(/[.,!?;:]+$/)
 
||
 
'')
 
:
 
'').join('
 
');
 
            }  
            return  t;  
        }  
 
        //  ---  INTERACTION  &  REHEARSAL  ---  

--- PAGE 155 ---
 
        function  handleLineClick(idx)  {  
            if(AppState.isSlashMode)  {  
                AppState.beatMarkers.has(idx)  ?  
AppState.beatMarkers.delete(idx)
 
:
 
AppState.beatMarkers.add(idx);
 
                renderScript();  
                return;  
            }  
            //  Peek  logic  
            const  el  =  document.querySelector(`#line-${idx}  
.ghost-text`);
 
            if(!el)  return;  
             
            if(!AppState.lineStats[idx])  
AppState.lineStats[idx]
 
=
 
{peeks:
 
0,
 
score:
 
0};
 
            AppState.lineStats[idx].peeks++;  
            AppState.lineStats[idx].score  =  Math.max(0,  
AppState.lineStats[idx].score
 
-
 
20);
 
             
            const  originalText  =  
AppState.rawText.split('\n').filter(l
 
=>
 
l.trim())[idx];
 
            const  currentDisplay  =  el.innerText;  

--- PAGE 156 ---
            el.innerText  =  originalText;  
            el.style.opacity  =  1;  
             
            setTimeout(()  =>  {  
                el.innerText  =  currentDisplay;  
                el.style.opacity  =  
document.getElementById('ghost-slider').value
 
/
 
100;
 
                renderScript();  //  Refresh  colors  
            },  2000);  
        }  
 
        function  toggleVoiceMode()  {  
            AppState.isVoiceActive  =  !AppState.isVoiceActive;  
            const  btn  =  
document.getElementById('master-voice-btn');
 
            btn.innerText  =  AppState.isVoiceActive  ?  "VOICE:  
ON"
 
:
 
"VOICE:
 
OFF";
 
            btn.classList.toggle('bg-red-600',  
AppState.isVoiceActive);
 
            if(AppState.isVoiceActive)  runVoiceLoop();  
        }  
 

--- PAGE 157 ---
        async  function  runVoiceLoop()  {  
            const  synth  =  window.speechSynthesis;  
            const  lines  =  
AppState.rawText.split('\n').filter(l
 
=>
 
l.trim());
 
             
            for(let  i  =  0;  i  <  lines.length;  i++)  {  
                if(!AppState.isVoiceActive)  break;  
                 
                const  type  =  getLineType(lines[i],  
lines[i].trim());
 
                const  isUser  =  
lines[i].includes(AppState.userChar);
 
 
                if(!isUser  &&  type  ===  'dialogue')  {  
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  {  
                        const  ut  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i]);
 
                        ut.onend  =  res;  
                        synth.speak(ut);  
                    });  
                }  else  if(isUser)  {  

--- PAGE 158 ---
                    //  Whisper  prompter  logic  could  be  added  
here
 
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  setTimeout(res,  
3000));
 
 
                }  
            }  
        }  
 
        function  toggleChaos()  {  
            AppState.isChaosActive  =  
document.getElementById('chaos-toggle').checked;
 
            const  overlay  =  
document.getElementById('chaos-overlay');
 
            if(AppState.isChaosActive)  {  
                AppState.chaosInterval  =  setInterval(()  =>  {  
                    overlay.style.opacity  =  "0.2";  
                    overlay.style.backgroundColor  =  
`hsl(${Math.random()*360},
 
70%,
 
50%)`;
 
                    setTimeout(()  =>  overlay.style.opacity  =  
"0",
 
100);
 
                },  2000);  
            }  else  {  
                clearInterval(AppState.chaosInterval);  

--- PAGE 159 ---
            }  
        }  
 
        //  ---  UTILS  ---  
        function  getLineType(line,  trimmed)  {  
            if(AppState.format  ===  'stageplay'  &&  
trimmed.match(/^[A-Z\s]+:/))
 
return
 
'character';
 
            if(AppState.format  ===  'screenplay'  &&  trimmed  ===  
trimmed.toUpperCase()
 
&&
 
!trimmed.match(/[.!?]/))
 
return
 
'character';
 
            if(trimmed.startsWith('(')  ||  
trimmed.startsWith('['))
 
return
 
'direction';
 
            return  'dialogue';  
        }  
 
        function  syncUI()  {  
            const  size  =  
document.getElementById('size-slider').value;
 
            
document.getElementById('script-display').style.fontSize
 
=
 
size
 
+
 
'px';
 
            const  ghost  =  
document.getElementById('ghost-slider').value;
 

--- PAGE 160 ---
            document.querySelectorAll('.is-user-line  
.ghost-text').forEach(el
 
=>
 
el.style.opacity
 
=
 
ghost/100);
 
        }

        }

Improvements  in  this  Final  Build:  
1.  Unified  State:  Every  toggle  (Punc,  Chaos,  ViewMode)  now  calls  a  central  renderScript() function.  This  prevents  the  tool  from  "forgetting"  you  were  in  
Mnemonic
 
mode
 
just
 
because
 
you
 
added
 
a
 
Beat
 
break.
 2.  Performance  Optimization:  We  use  a  virtualized-style  logic  for  shouldShowLine,  meaning  Ladder  and  Reverse  modes  are  now  incredibly  fast  
and
 
don't
 
require
 
re-parsing
 
the
 
text.
 3.  Visual  Polish:  Added  an  Aggr.  Clean  &  Import  button  that  gives  the  actor  
confidence
 
that
 
we've
 
scrubbed
 
the
 
"PDF
 
noise."
 4.  Heatmap  Integration:  The  background  of  each  line  now  reflects  its  lineStats.score.  As  you  practice,  your  script  will  literally  turn  green.  5.  Chaos  Engine:  Now  runs  as  an  overlay,  ensuring  it  doesn't  interrupt  text  
readability
 
but
 
provides
 
that
 
jarring
 
"set-like"
 
distraction.

    <script>  
        //  ---  DATA  PERSISTENCE  ENGINE  ---  
        const  SAVE_KEY  =  "LINELOCK_TITAN_V1";  
        let  AppState  =  {  
            raw:  "",  lines:  [],  user:  "",  viewMode:  'full',  
trainMode:
 
'standard',
 
 
            step:  1,  voice:  false,  chaos:  false,  slash:  false,  
punc:
 
true,
 
 
            beats:  [],  activeBeat:  null,  stats:  {},  format:  
'screenplay'
 
        };  
 

--- PAGE 173 ---
        function  save()  {   
            const  stateToSave  =  {...AppState,  beats:  
Array.from(AppState.beats)};
 
            localStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY,  
JSON.stringify(stateToSave));
 
 
        }  
 
        function  load()  {  
            const  data  =  localStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY);  
            if  (!data)  return;  
            const  parsed  =  JSON.parse(data);  
            AppState  =  {...parsed,  beats:  new  
Set(parsed.beats)};
 
            if(AppState.raw)  resumeSession();  
        }  
 
        //  ---  THE  ENGINE  ---  
        const  synth  =  window.speechSynthesis;  
        const  EmotionMap  =  {  
            neutral:  {r:  1,  p:  1,  v:  1},  
            aggressive:  {r:  1.4,  p:  0.8,  v:  1},  

--- PAGE 174 ---
            sarcastic:  {r:  0.8,  p:  0.7,  v:  0.9},  
            vulnerable:  {r:  0.7,  p:  1.2,  v:  0.6}  
        };  
 
        function  importScript()  {  
            let  t  =  
document.getElementById('script-input').value;
 
            if(!t.trim())  return;  
            //  Aggressive  Cleaner  
            AppState.raw  =  t.split('\n')  
                .filter(l  =>  !l.match(/^(\d+\.|Page  
\d+|\d{1,2}\/\d{2}\/\d{2,4}|\*|CONT'D)/i))
 
                .filter(l  =>  l.trim().length  >  0).join('\n');  
            detectFormat();  
            showCharSelect();  
            save();  
        }  
 
        function  resumeSession()  {  
            
document.getElementById('input-view').classList.add('hidden');
 

--- PAGE 175 ---
            if(!AppState.user)  showCharSelect();  
            else  showWork();  
        }  
 
        function  detectFormat()  {  
            AppState.format  =  
AppState.raw.slice(0,500).match(/[A-Z\s]+:/)
 
?
 
'stage'
 
:
 
'screen';
 
        }  
 
        function  showCharSelect()  {  
            
document.getElementById('input-view').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('char-view').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            const  chars  =  [...new  
Set(AppState.raw.match(/^[A-Z]{2,}(?=\s|$|:)/gm))];
 
            document.getElementById('char-grid').innerHTML  =  
chars.map(c
 
=>
 
`
 
                <button  onclick="setChar('${c}')"  class="px-8  
py-4
 
bg-white
 
border-2
 
rounded-2xl
 
font-black
 
text-slate-600
 
hover:border-indigo-600
 
hover:text-indigo-600
 
transition
 
shadow-sm">${c}</button>
 

--- PAGE 176 ---
            `).join('');  
        }  
 
        function  setChar(c)  {  AppState.user  =  c;  showWork();  
save();
 
}
 
 
        function  showWork()  {  
            
document.getElementById('char-view').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('work-view').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            render();  
        }  
 
        function  render()  {  
            const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
            const  lines  =  AppState.raw.split('\n');  
            container.innerHTML  =  '';  
             
            let  curChar  =  "";  

--- PAGE 177 ---
            let  curBeat  =  0;  
            const  g  =  
document.getElementById('g-slider').value
 
/
 
100;
 
            const  sz  =  
document.getElementById('s-slider').value;
 
            const  bl  =  
document.getElementById('b-slider').value;
 
 
            lines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
                const  trimmed  =  line.trim();  
                const  type  =  getLineType(line,  trimmed);  
                if(type  ===  'char')  curChar  =  
trimmed.replace(':','');
 
                if(AppState.beats.has(idx))  curBeat++;  
 
                //  Filter  logic  
                if(AppState.activeBeat  !==  null  &&  curBeat  !==  
AppState.activeBeat)
 
return;
 
                if(AppState.trainMode  ===  'ladder'  &&  idx  >  
AppState.step)
 
return;
 
                if(AppState.trainMode  ===  'reverse'  &&  idx  <  
(lines.length
 
-
 
AppState.step))
 
return;
 
 

--- PAGE 178 ---
                if(AppState.beats.has(idx))  {  
                    container.innerHTML  +=  `<div  class="py-12  
flex
 
items-center"><div
 
class="flex-grow
 
border-t-2
 
border-dashed
 
border-red-100"></div><span
 
class="px-4
 
text-[9px]
 
font-black
 
text-red-300
 
tracking-[0.5em]">BEAT
 
${curBeat+1}</span><div
 
class="flex-grow
 
border-t-2
 
border-dashed
 
border-red-100"></div></div>`;
 
                }  
 
                const  isU  =  (curChar  ===  AppState.user);  
                const  div  =  document.createElement('div');  
                div.className  =  `p-4  transition-all  
duration-300
 
${isU
 
?
 
'is-user-line
 
my-2'
 
:
 
'opacity-40
 
text-sm'}
 
${AppState.slash
 
?
 
'hover:bg-red-50
 
cursor-crosshair'
 
:
 
''}`;
 
                div.style.fontSize  =  sz  +  'px';  
                div.onclick  =  ()  =>  handleLine(idx);  
 
                //  Heatmap  
                const  st  =  AppState.stats[idx]  ||  {p:0,  s:0};  
                if(st.s  >  50)  div.style.backgroundColor  =  
`rgba(34,
 
197,
 
94,
 
0.08)`;
 
                if(st.p  >  3)  div.style.backgroundColor  =  
`rgba(239,
 
68,
 
68,
 
0.08)`;
 

--- PAGE 179 ---
 
                if(type  ===  'char')  div.innerHTML  =  `<span  
class="text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
text-indigo-400
 
block
 
mb-1
 
uppercase
 
tracking-widest">${curChar}</span>`;
 
                else  if(type  ===  'dir')  div.innerHTML  =  `<span  
class="italic
 
text-slate-400
 
opacity-60
 
text-xs">${trimmed}</span>`;
 
                else  {  
                    let  txt  =  trimmed;  
                    if(!AppState.punc)  txt  =  
txt.replace(/[^\w\s]|_/g,
 
"");
 
                    if(isU  &&  bl  >  0)  txt  =  txt.split('  
').map(w
 
=>
 
Math.random()*100
 
<
 
bl
 
?
 
'_____'
 
:
 
w).join('
 
');
 
                    if(isU  &&  AppState.viewMode  ===  
'mnemonic')
 
txt
 
=
 
txt.split('
 
').map(w
 
=>
 
w[0]
 
?
 
w[0]
 
+
 
(w.match(/[.,!?;:]+$/)
 
||
 
'')
 
:
 
'').join('
 
');
 
                     
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="ghost-text  
block"
 
style="opacity:${isU
 
?
 
g
 
:
 
1}">${txt}</span>`;
 
                }  
                container.appendChild(div);  
            });  
            renderBeatNav();  
        }  

--- PAGE 180 ---
 
        function  handleLine(idx)  {  
            if(AppState.slash)  {  
                AppState.beats.has(idx)  ?  
AppState.beats.delete(idx)
 
:
 
AppState.beats.add(idx);
 
                render();  save();  return;  
            }  
            const  st  =  AppState.stats[idx]  ||  {p:0,  s:0};  
            st.p++;  st.s  =  Math.max(0,  st.s  -  20);  
            AppState.stats[idx]  =  st;  
             
            const  el  =  document.querySelector(`#script-display  
>
 
div:nth-child(${Array.from(document.getElementById('script-dis
play').children).findIndex(n
 
=>
 
n.contains(event.target))
 
+
 
1})
 
.ghost-text`);
 
            if(!el)  return;  
            const  original  =  
AppState.raw.split('\n').filter(l=>l.trim())[idx];
 
            const  cur  =  el.innerText;  
            el.innerText  =  original;  el.style.opacity  =  1;  
            setTimeout(()  =>  {  el.innerText  =  cur;  
el.style.opacity
 
=
 

--- PAGE 181 ---
document.getElementById('g-slider').value/100;  render();  },  
2000);
 
            save();  
        }  
 
        function  getLineType(l,  t)  {  
            if(AppState.format  ===  'stage'  &&  
t.match(/^[A-Z\s]+:/))
 
return
 
'char';
 
            if(AppState.format  ===  'screen'  &&  t  ===  
t.toUpperCase()
 
&&
 
!t.match(/[.!?]/))
 
return
 
'char';
 
            if(t.startsWith('(')  ||  t.startsWith('['))  return  
'dir';
 
            return  'char_dialogue';  //  simple  fallback  
        }  
 
        //  ---  VOICE  ENGINE  ---  
        async  function  toggleVoiceMode()  {  
            AppState.voice  =  !AppState.voice;  
            
document.getElementById('voice-master-btn').innerText
 
=
 
AppState.voice
 
?
 
"VOICE:
 
ON"
 
:
 
"VOICE:
 
OFF";
 

--- PAGE 182 ---
            
document.getElementById('voice-master-btn').classList.toggle('
bg-red-600',
 
AppState.voice);
 
            if(AppState.voice)  runLoop();  
            else  synth.cancel();  
        }  
 
        async  function  runLoop()  {  
            const  lines  =  
AppState.raw.split('\n').filter(l=>l.trim());
 
            const  trig  =  
document.getElementById('trigger-mode').value;
 
            const  intention  =  
document.getElementById('voice-intention').value;
 
            const  cfg  =  EmotionMap[intention];  
 
            for(let  i  =  0;  i  <  lines.length;  i++)  {  
                if(!AppState.voice)  break;  
                const  isU  =  lines[i].includes(AppState.user);  
                 
                if(!isU)  {  
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  {  

--- PAGE 183 ---
                        const  ut  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i]);
 
                        ut.rate  =  cfg.r;  ut.pitch  =  cfg.p;  
ut.volume
 
=
 
cfg.v;
 
                        ut.onend  =  res;  synth.speak(ut);  
                    });  
                }  else  {  
                    //  Whisper  Prompter  
                    let  finished  =  false;  
                    
if(document.getElementById('whisper-toggle').checked)
 
{
 
                        setTimeout(()  =>  {  
                            if(!finished  &&  AppState.voice)  {  
                                const  whisper  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i].split('
 
').slice(0,3).join('
 
'));
 
                                whisper.volume  =  0.3;  
whisper.pitch
 
=
 
1.3;
 
synth.speak(whisper);
 
                            }  
                        },  5000);  
                    }  
 

--- PAGE 184 ---
                    if(trig  ===  'time')  await  new  Promise(res  
=>
 
setTimeout(res,
 
(lines[i].length
 
*
 
50)
 
+
 
2000));
 
                    else  if(trig  ===  'tap')  await  new  
Promise(res
 
=>
 
window.addEventListener('click',
 
res,
 
{once:true}));
 
                    else  if(trig  ===  'voice')  {  
                        //  Simulating  voice  trigger  for  
web-safety
 
                        await  new  Promise(res  =>  
setTimeout(res,
 
4000));
 
                    }  
                    finished  =  true;  
                }  
            }  
        }  
 
        //  ---  UI  UTILS  ---  
        function  toggleDashboard()  {  
            
document.getElementById('dash-modal').classList.toggle('hidden
');
 
            const  totalPeeks  =  
Object.values(AppState.stats).reduce((a,
 
b)
 
=>
 
a
 
+
 
b.p,
 
0);
 

--- PAGE 185 ---
            document.getElementById('stat-peeks').innerText  =  
totalPeeks;
 
            //  Confidence  is  average  of  scores  
            const  scores  =  Object.values(AppState.stats).map(s  
=>
 
s.s);
 
            const  avg  =  scores.length  ?  
Math.round(scores.reduce((a,b)=>a+b)/scores.length)
 
:
 
0;
 
            document.getElementById('stat-conf').innerText  =  
avg
 
+
 
"%";
 
        }  
 
        function  toggleChaos()  {  
            const  overlay  =  
document.getElementById('chaos-overlay');
 
            
if(document.getElementById('chaos-toggle').checked)
 
{
 
                AppState.chaosInt  =  setInterval(()  =>  {  
                    overlay.style.opacity  =  "0.15";  
overlay.style.backgroundColor
 
=
 
`hsl(${Math.random()*360},
 
100%,
 
50%)`;
 
                    setTimeout(()  =>  overlay.style.opacity  =  
"0",
 
150);
 
                },  1500);  
            }  else  clearInterval(AppState.chaosInt);  

--- PAGE 186 ---
        }  
 
        function  renderBeatNav()  {  
            const  nav  =  document.getElementById('beat-nav');  
            if(AppState.beats.size  ===  0)  return  nav.innerHTML  
=
 
"";
 
            const  markers  =  [0,  
...Array.from(AppState.beats).sort((a,b)=>a-b)];
 
            nav.innerHTML  =  `<button  onclick="setBeat(null)"  
class="px-4
 
py-1
 
rounded-full
 
text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
${AppState.activeBeat
 
===
 
null
 
?
 
'bg-indigo-600
 
text-white'
 
:
 
'bg-white
 
border
 
text-slate-400'}">FULL</button>`;
 
            markers.forEach((_,  i)  =>  {  
                nav.innerHTML  +=  `<button  
onclick="setBeat(${i})"
 
class="px-4
 
py-1
 
rounded-full
 
text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
${AppState.activeBeat
 
===
 
i
 
?
 
'bg-red-600
 
text-white'
 
:
 
'bg-white
 
border
 
text-slate-400'}">BEAT
 
${i+1}</button>`;
 
            });  
        }  
 
        function  setBeat(i)  {  AppState.activeBeat  =  i;  
render();
 
}
 
        function  setTrain(m)  {  AppState.trainMode  =  
(AppState.trainMode
 
===
 
m)
 
?
 
'standard'
 
:
 
m;
 
render();
 
}
 

--- PAGE 187 ---
        function  togglePunc()  {  AppState.punc  =  
!AppState.punc;
 
render();
 
}
 
        function  toggleSlash()  {  AppState.slash  =  
!AppState.slash;
 
render();
 
}
 
        function  clearAllData()  {  if(confirm("Wipe  
everything?"))
 
{
 
localStorage.clear();
 
location.reload();
 
}
 
}
 
 
        window.onload  =  load;  
    </script>  
</body>  
</html>

 AppState architecture  keeps  the  Raw  Text  intact  and  applies

        //  ---  PERSISTENCE  &  STATE  ---  
        const  DB_KEY  =  "LINELOCK_TITAN_PRO_2026";  
        let  State  =  {  
            raw:  "",  user:  "",  viewMode:  'full',  trainMode:  
'standard',
 
 
            step:  1,  voice:  false,  chaos:  false,  slash:  false,  
punc:
 
true,
 
 
            beats:  [],  activeBeat:  null,  stats:  {},  format:  
'screenplay'
 
        };  
 
        const  save  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  data  =  {...State,  beats:  
Array.from(State.beats)};
 
            localStorage.setItem(DB_KEY,  
JSON.stringify(data));
 
        };  

--- PAGE 204 ---
 
        const  load  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  data  =  localStorage.getItem(DB_KEY);  
            if(!data)  return;  
            const  parsed  =  JSON.parse(data);  
            State  =  {...parsed,  beats:  new  Set(parsed.beats)};  
            if(State.raw)  resume();  
        };  
 
        const  updateAppState  =  (key,  val)  =>  {  State[key]  =  
val;
 
render();
 
save();
 
};
 
 
        //  ---  PARSING  &  IMPORT  ---  
        const  importScript  =  ()  =>  {  
            let  t  =  
document.getElementById('script-input').value;
 
            if(!t.trim())  return;  
            //  Aggressive  Cleaner  Pass  
            State.raw  =  t.split('\n')  
                .filter(l  =>  !l.match(/^(\d+\.|Page  
\d+|\d{1,2}\/\d{2}\/\d{2,4}|\*|CONT'D|CONTINUED)/i))
 

--- PAGE 205 ---
                .filter(l  =>  l.trim().length  >  0).join('\n');  
             
            //  Format  Detection  
            State.format  =  
State.raw.slice(0,500).match(/[A-Z\s]+:/)
 
?
 
'stage'
 
:
 
'screen';
 
            document.getElementById('format-label').innerText  
=
 
State.format
 
===
 
'stage'
 
?
 
'Stageplay
 
Detected'
 
:
 
'Screenplay
 
Detected';
 
             
            showCharSelect();  
            save();  
        };  
 
        const  showCharSelect  =  ()  =>  {  
            
document.getElementById('view-input').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('view-char').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            const  chars  =  [...new  
Set(State.raw.match(/^[A-Z]{2,}(?=\s|$|:)/gm))];
 
            document.getElementById('char-grid').innerHTML  =  
chars.map(c
 
=>
 
`
 

--- PAGE 206 ---
                <button  onclick="setChar('${c}')"  class="px-8  
py-4
 
bg-white
 
border-2
 
rounded-3xl
 
font-black
 
text-slate-600
 
hover:border-indigo-600
 
hover:text-indigo-600
 
transition
 
shadow-sm
 
active:scale-95">${c}</button>
 
            `).join('');  
        };  
 
        const  setChar  =  (c)  =>  {  State.user  =  c;  resume();  };  
        const  resume  =  ()  =>  {  
            
document.getElementById('view-input').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('view-char').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('view-work').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            render();  
        };  
 
        //  ---  THE  TITAN  RENDERER  ---  
        const  render  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 

--- PAGE 207 ---
            const  lines  =  State.raw.split('\n');  
            container.innerHTML  =  '';  
             
            let  curChar  =  "";  
            let  curBeat  =  0;  
            const  g  =  
document.getElementById('g-slider').value
 
/
 
100;
 
            const  sz  =  
document.getElementById('s-slider').value;
 
            const  bl  =  
document.getElementById('b-slider').value;
 
 
            lines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
                const  trimmed  =  line.trim();  
                const  type  =  getLineType(line,  trimmed);  
                if(type  ===  'char')  curChar  =  
trimmed.replace(':','');
 
                if(State.beats.has(idx))  curBeat++;  
 
                //  Progressive  Filters  (Ladder/Reverse/Beat  
Isolation)
 
                if(State.activeBeat  !==  null  &&  curBeat  !==  
State.activeBeat)
 
return;
 

--- PAGE 208 ---
                if(State.trainMode  ===  'ladder'  &&  idx  >  
State.step)
 
return;
 
                if(State.trainMode  ===  'reverse'  &&  idx  <  
(lines.length
 
-
 
State.step))
 
return;
 
 
                //  Visual  Beat  Dividers  
                if(State.beats.has(idx))  {  
                    const  sep  =  document.createElement('div');  
                    sep.className  =  "py-8  flex  items-center";  
                    sep.innerHTML  =  `<div  class="flex-grow  
border-t-2
 
border-dashed
 
border-red-100"></div><span
 
class="px-4
 
text-[8px]
 
font-black
 
text-red-300
 
tracking-[0.5em]">BEAT
 
${curBeat+1}</span><div
 
class="flex-grow
 
border-t-2
 
border-dashed
 
border-red-100"></div>`;
 
                    container.appendChild(sep);  
                }  
 
                const  isU  =  (curChar  ===  State.user);  
                const  div  =  document.createElement('div');  
                div.id  =  `line-${idx}`;  
                div.className  =  `line-container  p-4  
transition-all
 
duration-300
 
${isU
 
?
 
'is-user-line
 
my-2'
 
:
 

--- PAGE 209 ---
'opacity-40  text-sm'}  ${State.slash  ?  'cursor-crosshair  
border-2
 
border-dashed
 
border-red-200'
 
:
 
''}`;
 
                div.style.fontSize  =  sz  +  'px';  
                div.onclick  =  ()  =>  handleLineAction(idx);  
 
                //  Heatmap  Confidence  Overlay  
                const  st  =  State.stats[idx]  ||  {p:0,  s:0};  
                if(st.s  >  60)  
div.classList.add('heatmap-green');
 
                if(st.p  >  3)  div.classList.add('heatmap-red');  
 
                if(type  ===  'char')  {  
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="text-[9px]  
font-black
 
text-indigo-400
 
block
 
mb-1
 
uppercase
 
tracking-widest">${curChar}</span>`;
 
                }  else  if(type  ===  'dir')  {  
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="italic  
text-slate-400
 
opacity-60
 
text-xs">${trimmed}</span>`;
 
                }  else  {  
                    let  txt  =  trimmed;  
                    if(!State.punc)  txt  =  
txt.replace(/[^\w\s]|_/g,
 
"");
 

--- PAGE 210 ---
                    if(isU  &&  bl  >  0)  txt  =  txt.split('  
').map(w
 
=>
 
Math.random()*100
 
<
 
bl
 
?
 
'_____'
 
:
 
w).join('
 
');
 
                    if(isU  &&  State.viewMode  ===  'mnemonic')  
txt
 
=
 
txt.split('
 
').map(w
 
=>
 
w[0]
 
?
 
w[0]
 
+
 
(w.match(/[.,!?;:]+$/)
 
||
 
'')
 
:
 
'').join('
 
');
 
                     
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="ghost-text  
block"
 
style="opacity:${isU
 
?
 
g
 
:
 
1}">${txt}</span>`;
 
                }  
                container.appendChild(div);  
            });  
            renderBeatNav();  
            syncModeUI();  
        };  
 
        const  handleLineAction  =  (idx)  =>  {  
            if(State.slash)  {  
                State.beats.has(idx)  ?  State.beats.delete(idx)  
:
 
State.beats.add(idx);
 
                render();  save();  return;  
            }  
            const  st  =  State.stats[idx]  ||  {p:0,  s:0};  

--- PAGE 211 ---
            st.p++;  st.s  =  Math.max(0,  st.s  -  25);  
            State.stats[idx]  =  st;  
             
            //  Advanced  "Peek"  Interaction  
            const  lineEl  =  
document.getElementById(`line-${idx}`);
 
            const  textEl  =  
lineEl.querySelector('.ghost-text');
 
            if(!textEl)  return;  
            const  original  =  
State.raw.split('\n').filter(l=>l.trim())[idx];
 
            const  current  =  textEl.innerText;  
             
            textEl.innerText  =  original;  
            textEl.style.opacity  =  1;  
            lineEl.classList.add('active-drill');  
             
            setTimeout(()  =>  {  
                textEl.innerText  =  current;  
                textEl.style.opacity  =  
document.getElementById('g-slider').value
 
/
 
100;
 
                lineEl.classList.remove('active-drill');  

--- PAGE 212 ---
                render();  
            },  1800);  
            save();  
        };  
 
        //  ---  SOLO  PARTNER  &  TTS  ---  
        const  synth  =  window.speechSynthesis;  
        const  VoiceLibrary  =  {  
            neutral:  {r:  1,  p:  1,  v:  1},  
            aggressive:  {r:  1.4,  p:  0.8,  v:  1},  
            sarcastic:  {r:  0.8,  p:  0.7,  v:  1},  
            vulnerable:  {r:  0.7,  p:  1.2,  v:  0.6}  
        };  
 
        const  toggleVoiceMode  =  ()  =>  {  
            State.voice  =  !State.voice;  
            const  btn  =  
document.getElementById('voice-master-btn');
 
            btn.innerText  =  State.voice  ?  "VOICE:  ON"  :  
"VOICE:
 
OFF";
 
            btn.classList.toggle('bg-red-600',  State.voice);  

--- PAGE 213 ---
            if(State.voice)  loopLines();  else  synth.cancel();  
        };  
 
        async  function  loopLines()  {  
            const  lines  =  
State.raw.split('\n').filter(l=>l.trim());
 
            const  tMode  =  
document.getElementById('t-mode').value;
 
            const  intention  =  
document.getElementById('v-intention').value;
 
            const  config  =  VoiceLibrary[intention];  
 
            for(let  i  =  0;  i  <  lines.length;  i++)  {  
                if(!State.voice)  break;  
                const  isU  =  lines[i].includes(State.user);  
                 
                if(!isU)  {  
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  {  
                        const  ut  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i]);
 
                        ut.rate  =  config.r;  ut.pitch  =  
config.p;
 
ut.volume
 
=
 
config.v;
 

--- PAGE 214 ---
                        ut.onend  =  res;  synth.speak(ut);  
                    });  
                }  else  {  
                    //  Whisper  Prompter  Logic  
                    let  solved  =  false;  
                    
if(document.getElementById('whisper-toggle').checked)
 
{
 
                        setTimeout(()  =>  {  
                            if(!solved  &&  State.voice)  {  
                                const  hint  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i].split('
 
').slice(0,3).join('
 
'));
 
                                hint.volume  =  0.3;  hint.pitch  
=
 
1.4;
 
synth.speak(hint);
 
                            }  
                        },  5000);  
                    }  
 
                    if(tMode  ===  'time')  await  new  Promise(res  
=>
 
setTimeout(res,
 
(lines[i].length
 
*
 
60)
 
+
 
1500));
 
                    else  if(tMode  ===  'tap')  await  new  
Promise(res
 
=>
 
window.addEventListener('click',
 
res,
 
{once:true}));
 

--- PAGE 215 ---
                    else  if(tMode  ===  'voice')  await  new  
Promise(res
 
=>
 
setTimeout(res,
 
4000));
 
//
 
Simulated
 
fallback
 
                    solved  =  true;  
                }  
            }  
        }  
 
        //  ---  UTILITIES  ---  
        const  getLineType  =  (l,  t)  =>  {  
            if(State.format  ===  'stage'  &&  
t.match(/^[A-Z\s]+:/))
 
return
 
'char';
 
            if(State.format  ===  'screen'  &&  t  ===  
t.toUpperCase()
 
&&
 
!t.match(/[.!?]/))
 
return
 
'char';
 
            if(t.startsWith('(')  ||  t.startsWith('['))  return  
'dir';
 
            return  'dialogue';  
        };  
 
        const  renderBeatNav  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  nav  =  document.getElementById('beat-nav');  
            if(State.beats.size  ===  0)  return  nav.innerHTML  =  
"";
 

--- PAGE 216 ---
            const  sorted  =  [0,  
...Array.from(State.beats).sort((a,b)=>a-b)];
 
            nav.innerHTML  =  `<button  onclick="setBeat(null)"  
class="px-4
 
py-2
 
rounded-2xl
 
text-[9px]
 
font-black
 
flex-shrink-0
 
${State.activeBeat
 
===
 
null
 
?
 
'bg-indigo-600
 
text-white
 
shadow-lg
 
shadow-indigo-200'
 
:
 
'bg-white
 
border
 
text-slate-400'}">FULL
 
SCRIPT</button>`;
 
            sorted.forEach((_,  i)  =>  {  
                nav.innerHTML  +=  `<button  
onclick="setBeat(${i})"
 
class="px-4
 
py-2
 
rounded-2xl
 
text-[9px]
 
font-black
 
flex-shrink-0
 
${State.activeBeat
 
===
 
i
 
?
 
'bg-red-600
 
text-white
 
shadow-lg
 
shadow-red-200'
 
:
 
'bg-white
 
border
 
text-slate-400'}">BEAT
 
${i+1}</button>`;
 
            });  
        };  
 
        const  toggleChaos  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  overlay  =  
document.getElementById('chaos-overlay');
 
            
if(document.getElementById('chaos-toggle').checked)
 
{
 
                State.chaosInt  =  setInterval(()  =>  {  
                    overlay.style.opacity  =  "0.2";  
                    overlay.style.backgroundColor  =  
`hsl(${Math.random()*360},
 
100%,
 
50%)`;
 

--- PAGE 217 ---
                    setTimeout(()  =>  overlay.style.opacity  =  
"0",
 
150);
 
                },  2000);  
            }  else  clearInterval(State.chaosInt);  
        };  
 
        const  toggleDashboard  =  ()  =>  {  
            
document.getElementById('dash-modal').classList.toggle('hidden
');
 
            const  peeks  =  
Object.values(State.stats).reduce((a,b)
 
=>
 
a
 
+
 
b.p,
 
0);
 
            document.getElementById('stat-peeks').innerText  =

            const  scores  =  Object.values(State.stats).map(s  =>

            const  avg  =  scores.length  ?

            document.getElementById('stat-conf').innerText  =

+

        };  
 
        const  setBeat  =  (i)  =>  {  State.activeBeat  =  i;

};
 

--- PAGE 218 ---
        const  setTrain  =  (m)  =>  {   
            State.trainMode  =  (State.trainMode  ===  m)  ?  
'standard'
 
:

            State.step  =  1;  render();   
        };  
        const  togglePunc  =  ()  =>  {  State.punc  =  !State.punc;

        const  toggleSlashMode  =  ()  =>  {  State.slash  =  
!State.slash;

        const  clearData  =  ()  =>  {  if(confirm("Reset

};
 
        const  syncModeUI  =  ()  =>  {

igo-600',

        };  
 

--- PAGE 219 ---
        //  Initialize  
        window.onload  =  load;  
        //  Navigation  failsafe  
        document.addEventListener('keydown',  (e)  =>  {  
            if(e.key  ===  "ArrowRight"  &&  State.trainMode  !==

            if(e.key  ===  "ArrowLeft"  &&  State.trainMode  !==

I.  The  "50  Point"  Fix  List  (Key  Highlights)

    <script>  
        pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc  =  
'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.wo
rker.min.js';
 
 
        let  State  =  {  
            raw:  "",  lines:  [],  userChar:  "",  isVoiceActive:  
false,
 
            stats:  {},  format:  'screenplay'  
        };  
 
        //  ---  1.  THE  PDF  ENGINE  (CRITICAL  FIX)  ---  
        async  function  handleFileUpload(event)  {  
            const  file  =  event.target.files[0];  
            if  (!file)  return;  
 
            
document.getElementById('loading-bar').classList.remove('hidde
n');
 

--- PAGE 228 ---
            const  reader  =  new  FileReader();  
            reader.onload  =  async  function()  {  
                const  typedarray  =  new  
Uint8Array(this.result);
 
                const  pdf  =  await  
pdfjsLib.getDocument(typedarray).promise;
 
                let  fullText  =  "";  
 
                for  (let  i  =  1;  i  <=  pdf.numPages;  i++)  {  
                    const  page  =  await  pdf.getPage(i);  
                    const  content  =  await  
page.getTextContent();
 
                    const  strings  =  content.items.map(item  =>  
item.str);
 
                    fullText  +=  strings.join("  ")  +  "\n";  
                    
document.getElementById('progress').style.width
 
=
 
`${(i
 
/
 
pdf.numPages)
 
*
 
100}%`;
 
                }  
                importText(fullText);  
            };  
            reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);  
        }  

--- PAGE 229 ---
 
        function  importText(text)  {  
            //  Aggressive  Cleaner  logic  
            State.raw  =  text.split('\n')  
                .map(l  =>  l.trim())  
                .filter(l  =>  !l.match(/^(\d+|Page  
\d+|\*|CONT'D)/i))
 
                .filter(l  =>  l.length  >  0)  
                .join('\n');  
 
            
document.getElementById('upload-view').classList.add('hidden')
;
 
            
document.getElementById('paste-view').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('char-view').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            
document.getElementById('status-indicator').innerText
 
=
 
"SCRIPT
 
LOADED";
 
             
            detectCharacters();  

--- PAGE 230 ---
        }  
 
        function  detectCharacters()  {  
            const  chars  =  [...new  
Set(State.raw.match(/^[A-Z\s]{2,}(?=:|\n|$)/gm))];
 
            const  grid  =  document.getElementById('char-grid');  
            grid.innerHTML  =  chars.map(c  =>  `  
                <button  onclick="setRole('${c.trim()}')"  
class="px-6
 
py-3
 
bg-white
 
border
 
rounded-2xl
 
font-bold
 
hover:border-indigo-600
 
transition">${c.trim()}</button>
 
            `).join('');  
        }  
 
        function  setRole(char)  {  
            State.userChar  =  char;  
            
document.getElementById('char-view').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('work-view').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            render();  
        }  
 

--- PAGE 231 ---
        //  ---  2.  THE  VOICE  ENGINE  (CRITICAL  FIX)  ---  
        function  loadVoices()  {  
            const  select  =  
document.getElementById('voice-select');
 
            const  voices  =  window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();  
            select.innerHTML  =  voices  
                .filter(v  =>  v.lang.includes('en'))  
                .map(v  =>  `<option  
value="${v.name}">${v.name}</option>`).join('');
 
        }  
        window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged  =  loadVoices;  
 
        function  toggleVoice()  {  
            State.isVoiceActive  =  !State.isVoiceActive;  
            const  btn  =  document.getElementById('voice-btn');  
            btn.innerText  =  State.isVoiceActive  ?  "STOP  VOICE"  
:
 
"START
 
VOICE";
 
            btn.classList.toggle('bg-red-600',  
State.isVoiceActive);
 
            if(State.isVoiceActive)  runLoop();  else  
window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
 
        }  

--- PAGE 232 ---
 
        async  function  runLoop()  {  
            const  lines  =  State.raw.split('\n');  
            const  intention  =  
document.getElementById('emotion-mode').value;
 
             
            for(let  i  =  0;  i  <  lines.length;  i++)  {  
                if(!State.isVoiceActive)  break;  
                const  line  =  lines[i].trim();  
                const  isUser  =  
line.startsWith(State.userChar);
 
 
                if(!isUser)  {  
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  {  
                        const  ut  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(line);
 
                        ut.voice  =  
window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().find(v
 
=>
 
v.name
 
===
 
document.getElementById('voice-select').value);
 
                         
                        //  Emotion  Toning  
                        if(intention  ===  'aggressive')  {  
ut.rate
 
=
 
1.4;
 
ut.pitch
 
=
 
0.8;
 
}
 

--- PAGE 233 ---
                        if(intention  ===  'vulnerable')  {  
ut.rate
 
=
 
0.8;
 
ut.pitch
 
=
 
1.2;
 
}
 
                         
                        ut.onend  =  res;  
                        window.speechSynthesis.speak(ut);  
                    });  
                }  else  {  
                    //  Wait  for  user  to  read  their  line  
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  setTimeout(res,  
line.length
 
*
 
70
 
+
 
2000));
 
                }  
            }  
        }  
 
        //  ---  3.  THE  RENDER  ENGINE  ---  
        function  render()  {  
            const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
            const  lines  =  State.raw.split('\n');  
            const  mode  =  
document.getElementById('view-mode').value;
 
            container.innerHTML  =  '';  

--- PAGE 234 ---
 
            lines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
                const  isUser  =  
line.trim().startsWith(State.userChar);
 
                const  div  =  document.createElement('div');  
                div.className  =  `p-4  rounded-xl  transition  
${isU
 
?
 
'is-user-line'
 
:
 
'opacity-40'}`;
 
                 
                let  text  =  line.trim();  
                if(isUser  &&  mode  ===  'mnemonic')  {  
                    text  =  text.split('  ').map(w  =>  w[0]  ?  
w[0]
 
:
 
'').join('
 
');
 
                }  
                 
                div.innerHTML  =  `<span  
class="ghost-text">${text}</span>`;
 
                container.appendChild(div);  
            });  
        }  
 
        function  togglePasteMode()  {  

--- PAGE 235 ---
            
document.getElementById('upload-view').classList.add('hidden')
;
 
            
document.getElementById('paste-view').classList.remove('hidden
');
 
        }
