    <script>  
        pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc  =  
'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.wo
rker.min.js';
 
 
        let  AppState  =  {  
            raw:  "",  lines:  [],  user:  "",  viewMode:  'full',  
trainMode:
 
'standard',
 
 
            step:  1,  voice:  false,  chaos:  false,  slash:  false,  
punc:
 
true,
 
 
            beats:  new  Set(),  activeBeat:  null,  stats:  {},  
format:
 
'screenplay',
 
scrollInterval:
 
null
 
        };  
 
        //  ---  1.  PERSISTENCE  ENGINE  ---  
        const  save  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  data  =  {...AppState,  beats:  
Array.from(AppState.beats)};
 
            localStorage.setItem("LINELOCK_TITAN_PRO",  
JSON.stringify(data));
 
        };  

--- PAGE 250 ---
        const  load  =  ()  =>  {  
            const  data  =  
localStorage.getItem("LINELOCK_TITAN_PRO");
 
            if(data)  {  
                const  parsed  =  JSON.parse(data);  
                AppState  =  {...parsed,  beats:  new  
Set(parsed.beats)};
 
                if(AppState.raw)  hydrate();  
            }  
        };  
 
        //  ---  2.  AGGRESSIVE  PDF  &  TEXT  PARSER  ---  
        async  function  handlePDF(e)  {  
            const  file  =  e.target.files[0];  
            if(!file)  return;  
             
            
document.getElementById('pdf-loader').classList.remove('hidden
');
 
            const  reader  =  new  FileReader();  
            reader.onload  =  async  function()  {  

--- PAGE 251 ---
                const  typedarray  =  new  
Uint8Array(this.result);
 
                const  pdf  =  await  
pdfjsLib.getDocument(typedarray).promise;
 
                let  fullText  =  "";  
                for(let  i  =  1;  i  <=  pdf.numPages;  i++){  
                    const  page  =  await  pdf.getPage(i);  
                    const  content  =  await  
page.getTextContent();
 
                    fullText  +=  content.items.map(item  =>  
item.str).join("
 
")
 
+
 
"\n";
 
                    
document.getElementById('pdf-progress').style.width
 
=
 
(i/pdf.numPages*100)
 
+
 
"%";
 
                }  
                importRaw(fullText);  
            };  
            reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);  
        }  
 
        function  importRaw(text)  {  
            //  Aggressive  cleaning  pass  
            AppState.raw  =  text.split('\n')  

--- PAGE 252 ---
                .map(l  =>  l.trim())  
                .filter(l  =>  !l.match(/^(\d+|Page  \d+|[A-Z\s]+  
\d{1,2}\/\d{2}\/\d{2,4}|\*|CONTINUED)/i))
 
                .filter(l  =>  l.length  >  0)  
                .join('\n');  
             
            //  Detect  Screenplay  vs  Stageplay  
            AppState.format  =  
(AppState.raw.slice(0,1000).match(/[A-Z\s]+:/))
 
?
 
'stage'
 
:
 
'screen';
 
             
            
document.getElementById('view-upload').classList.add('hidden')
;
 
            
document.getElementById('view-paste').classList.add('hidden');
 
            showCharacterSelect();  
            save();  
        }  
 
        function  showCharacterSelect()  {  

--- PAGE 253 ---
            
document.getElementById('view-char').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            //  Advanced  Name  Matching  
            const  regex  =  AppState.format  ===  'stage'  ?  
/^[A-Z\s]{2,}(?=:)/gm
 
:
 
/^[A-Z\s]{2,}(?=\n|$)/gm;
 
            const  chars  =  [...new  
Set(AppState.raw.match(regex))].map(c
 
=>
 
c.trim());
 
             
            document.getElementById('char-grid').innerHTML  =  
chars.map(c
 
=>
 
`
 
                <button  onclick="setRole('${c}')"  class="px-10  
py-5
 
bg-white
 
border-2
 
rounded-[2rem]
 
font-black
 
text-slate-700
 
hover:border-indigo-600
 
transition
 
active:scale-95
 
shadow-sm">${c}</button>
 
            `).join('');  
        }  
 
        function  setRole(c)  {  AppState.user  =  c;  hydrate();  }  
 
        function  hydrate()  {  
            
document.getElementById('view-upload').classList.add('hidden')
;
 

--- PAGE 254 ---
            
document.getElementById('view-char').classList.add('hidden');
 
            
document.getElementById('view-work').classList.remove('hidden'
);
 
            
document.getElementById('main-console').classList.remove('hidd
en');
 
            
document.getElementById('action-dock').classList.remove('hidde
n');
 
            render();  
        }  
 
        //  ---  3.  THE  TITAN  RENDERER  (ALL  FEATURES  INTEGRATED)  
---
 
        function  render()  {  
            const  container  =  
document.getElementById('script-display');
 
            const  lines  =  AppState.raw.split('\n');  
            container.innerHTML  =  '';  
             
            let  curChar  =  "";  
            let  curBeat  =  0;  

--- PAGE 255 ---
            const  g  =  
document.getElementById('g-slider').value
 
/
 
100;
 
            const  b  =  
document.getElementById('b-slider').value;
 
            const  sz  =  
document.getElementById('s-slider').value;
 
 
            lines.forEach((line,  idx)  =>  {  
                const  trimmed  =  line.trim();  
                const  type  =  getLineType(line,  trimmed);  
                if(type  ===  'char')  curChar  =  
trimmed.replace(':','').trim();
 
                if(AppState.beats.has(idx))  curBeat++;  
 
                //  Logic  Filters  
                if(AppState.activeBeat  !==  null  &&  curBeat  !==  
AppState.activeBeat)
 
return;
 
                if(AppState.trainMode  ===  'ladder'  &&  idx  >  
AppState.step)
 
return;
 
                if(AppState.trainMode  ===  'reverse'  &&  idx  <  
(lines.length
 
-
 
AppState.step))
 
return;
 
 
                if(AppState.beats.has(idx))  {  

--- PAGE 256 ---
                    container.innerHTML  +=  `<div  
class="beat-divider"><span
 
class="bg-slate-50
 
px-6
 
text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
text-red-400
 
tracking-[0.5em]">BEAT
 
${curBeat+1}</span></div>`;
 
                }  
 
                const  isU  =  (curChar  ===  AppState.user);  
                const  div  =  document.createElement('div');  
                div.id  =  `line-${idx}`;  
                div.className  =  `heatmap-cell  p-5  
transition-all
 
duration-300
 
${isU
 
?
 
'is-user-line
 
my-4'
 
:
 
'opacity-40
 
text-sm'}
 
${AppState.slash
 
?
 
'cursor-crosshair
 
border-2
 
border-dashed
 
border-red-200'
 
:
 
''}`;
 
                div.style.fontSize  =  sz  +  'px';  
                div.onclick  =  ()  =>  handleInteraction(idx);  
 
                //  Heatmap  Score  
                const  st  =  AppState.stats[idx]  ||  {p:0,  s:0};  
                if(st.s  >  50)  div.style.backgroundColor  =  
`rgba(16,
 
185,
 
129,
 
0.08)`;
 
                if(st.p  >  3)  div.style.backgroundColor  =  
`rgba(239,
 
68,
 
68,
 
0.08)`;
 
 

--- PAGE 257 ---
                if(type  ===  'char')  {  
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="text-[10px]  
font-black
 
text-indigo-500
 
uppercase
 
tracking-widest">${curChar}</span>`;
 
                }  else  if(type  ===  'dir')  {  
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="italic  
text-slate-400
 
opacity-60
 
text-xs">${trimmed}</span>`;
 
                }  else  {  
                    let  txt  =  trimmed;  
                    if(!AppState.punc)  txt  =  
txt.replace(/[^\w\s]|_/g,
 
"");
 
                    if(isU  &&  b  >  0)  txt  =  txt.split('  
').map(w
 
=>
 
Math.random()*100
 
<
 
b
 
?
 
'_____'
 
:
 
w).join('
 
');
 
                    if(isU  &&  AppState.viewMode  ===  
'mnemonic')
 
txt
 
=
 
txt.split('
 
').map(w
 
=>
 
w[0]
 
?
 
w[0]
 
+
 
(w.match(/[.,!?;:]+$/)
 
||
 
'')
 
:
 
'').join('
 
');
 
                     
                    div.innerHTML  =  `<span  class="ghost-text  
block"
 
style="opacity:${isU
 
?
 
g
 
:
 
1}">${txt}</span>`;
 
                }  
                container.appendChild(div);  
            });  
            updateBeatTabs();  

--- PAGE 258 ---
        }  
 
        //  ---  4.  SOLO  PARTNER  &  EMOTION  ENGINE  ---  
        const  synth  =  window.speechSynthesis;  
        const  EmotionMap  =  {  
            neutral:  {r:1,  p:1,  v:1},  aggressive:  {r:1.4,  
p:0.8,
 
v:1},
 
 
            sarcastic:  {r:0.8,  p:0.7,  v:0.9},  vulnerable:  
{r:0.7,
 
p:1.2,
 
v:0.6}
 
        };  
 
        async  function  toggleVoiceMode()  {  
            AppState.voice  =  !AppState.voice;  
            
document.getElementById('voice-master-btn').innerText
 
=
 
AppState.voice
 
?
 
"VOICE:
 
ON"
 
:
 
"VOICE:
 
OFF";
 
            
document.getElementById('voice-master-btn').classList.toggle('
bg-red-600',
 
AppState.voice);
 
            if(AppState.voice)  rehearsalLoop();  else  
synth.cancel();
 
        }  
 

--- PAGE 259 ---
        async  function  rehearsalLoop()  {  
            const  lines  =  AppState.raw.split('\n').filter(l  =>  
l.trim());
 
            const  trigger  =  
document.getElementById('t-mode').value;
 
            const  config  =  
EmotionMap[document.getElementById('v-intention').value];
 
             
            for(let  i  =  0;  i  <  lines.length;  i++)  {  
                if(!AppState.voice)  break;  
                const  isU  =  lines[i].includes(AppState.user);  
                 
                if(!isU)  {  
                    await  new  Promise(res  =>  {  
                        const  ut  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i]);
 
                        ut.rate  =  config.r;  ut.pitch  =  
config.p;
 
ut.onend
 
=
 
res;
 
                        synth.speak(ut);  
                    });  
                }  else  {  
                    //  Whisper  Prompter  

--- PAGE 260 ---
                    let  done  =  false;  
                    
if(document.getElementById('whisper-toggle').checked)
 
{
 
                        setTimeout(()  =>  {  
                            if(!done  &&  AppState.voice)  {  
                                const  hint  =  new  
SpeechSynthesisUtterance(lines[i].split('
 
').slice(0,3).join('
 
'));
 
                                hint.volume  =  0.3;  hint.pitch  
=
 
1.3;
 
synth.speak(hint);
 
                            }  
                        },  5000);  
                    }  
                    if(trigger  ===  'time')  await  new  
Promise(res
 
=>
 
setTimeout(res,
 
(lines[i].length
 
*
 
60)
 
+
 
2000));
 
                    else  await  new  Promise(res  =>  
window.addEventListener('click',
 
res,
 
{once:true}));
 
                    done  =  true;  
                }  
            }  
        }  
 

--- PAGE 261 ---
        //  ---  5.  INTERACTION  LOGIC  ---  
        function  handleInteraction(idx)  {  
            if(AppState.slash)  {  
                AppState.beats.has(idx)  ?  
AppState.beats.delete(idx)
 
:
 
AppState.beats.add(idx);
 
                render();  save();  return;  
            }  
            //  PEEK  LOGIC  
            const  st  =  AppState.stats[idx]  ||  {p:0,  s:0};  
            st.p++;  st.s  =  Math.max(0,  st.s  -  20);  
            AppState.stats[idx]  =  st;  
             
            const  lineEl  =  
document.getElementById(`line-${idx}`);
 
            const  textEl  =  
lineEl.querySelector('.ghost-text');
 
            if(!textEl)  return;  
            const  original  =  
AppState.raw.split('\n').filter(l=>l.trim())[idx];
 
            const  cur  =  textEl.innerText;  
            textEl.innerText  =  original;  textEl.style.opacity  
=
 
1;
 

--- PAGE 262 ---
            setTimeout(()  =>  {  textEl.innerText  =  cur;  
textEl.style.opacity
 
=
 
document.getElementById('g-slider').value/100;
 
render();
 
},
 
1800);
 
            save();  
        }  
 
        //  ---  6.  UTILS  &  UI  ---  
        function  getLineType(l,  t)  {  
            if(AppState.format  ===  'stage'  &&  
t.match(/^[A-Z\s]+:/))
 
return
 
'char';
 
            if(AppState.format  ===  'screen'  &&  t  ===  
t.toUpperCase()
 
&&
 
!t.match(/[.!?]/))
 
return
 
'char';
 
            return  (t.startsWith('(')  ||  t.startsWith('['))  ?  
'dir'
 
:
 
'dialogue';
 
        }  
 
        function  handleScrollChange()  {  
            const  val  =  
document.getElementById('scroll-slider').value;
 
            clearInterval(AppState.scrollInterval);  
            if(val  >  0)  {  

--- PAGE 263 ---
                AppState.scrollInterval  =  setInterval(()  =>  
window.scrollBy(0,
 
val
 
/
 
10),
 
30);
 
            }  
        }  
 
        function  toggleChaos()  {  
            const  overlay  =  
document.getElementById('chaos-overlay');
 
            
if(document.getElementById('chaos-toggle').checked)
 
{
 
                AppState.chaosTimer  =  setInterval(()  =>  {  
                    overlay.style.opacity  =  "0.2";  
                    overlay.style.backgroundColor  =  
`hsl(${Math.random()*360},
 
100%,
 
50%)`;
 
                    setTimeout(()  =>  overlay.style.opacity  =  
"0",
 
150);
 
                },  1500);  
            }  else  clearInterval(AppState.chaosTimer);  
        }  
 
        function  updateBeatTabs()  {  
            const  nav  =  document.getElementById('beat-tabs');  

--- PAGE 264 ---
            if(AppState.beats.size  ===  0)  return  nav.innerHTML  
=
 
"";
 
            const  markers  =  [0,  
...Array.from(AppState.beats).sort((a,b)=>a-b)];
 
            nav.innerHTML  =  `<button  onclick="setBeat(null)"  
class="px-6
 
py-2
 
rounded-full
 
text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
flex-shrink-0
 
${AppState.activeBeat
 
===
 
null
 
?
 
'bg-indigo-600
 
text-white
 
shadow-xl'
 
:
 
'bg-white
 
border
 
text-slate-400'}">FULL
 
SCRIPT</button>`;
 
            markers.forEach((_,  i)  =>  {  
                nav.innerHTML  +=  `<button  
onclick="setBeat(${i})"
 
class="px-6
 
py-2
 
rounded-full
 
text-[10px]
 
font-black
 
flex-shrink-0
 
${AppState.activeBeat
 
===
 
i
 
?
 
'bg-red-600
 
text-white
 
shadow-xl'
 
:
 
'bg-white
 
border
 
text-slate-400'}">BEAT
 
${i+1}</button>`;
 
            });  
        }  
 
        function  setBeat(i)  {  AppState.activeBeat  =  i;  
render();
 
}
 
        function  setTrain(m)  {  AppState.trainMode  =  
(AppState.trainMode
 
===

?
 
'standard'
 
:

AppState.step
 
=

}
 
        function  togglePunc()  {  AppState.punc  =  
!AppState.punc;

--- PAGE 265 ---
        function  toggleSlashMode()  {  AppState.slash  =  
!AppState.slash;
 
document.getElementById('slash-btn').classList.toggle('text-re

}
 
        function  toggleDashboard()  {

Why  this  is  the  "Titan"  you  were  looking  for:  
1.  PDF.js  Integration:  It  doesn't  just  "ask"  for  a  PDF;  it  uses  a  multi-threaded  
worker
 
to
 
extract
 
text,
 
showing
 
a
 
progress
 
bar
 
as
 
it
 
goes.
 2.  State  Management:  Every  variable  is  tied  to  the  AppState.  If  you  cut  a  beat,  the  
Mnemonic
 
mode
 
and
 
the
 
Emotion
 
settings
 
remain
 
locked
 
in.
 3.  Advanced  Cleanup:  It  doesn't  just  strip  text.  It  targets  specifically  formatted  
"Page
 
X"
 
or
 
"Date
 
Revision"
 
lines
 
and
 
removes
 
them,
 
so
 
they
 
don't
 
appear
 
in
 
your
 
line-running.
 4.  The  "Whisper"  Safety  Net:  In  Voice  mode,  if  you  stall  for  5  seconds,  it  uses  a  
high-pitched,
 
low-volume
 
"Whisper"
 
to
 
give
 
you
 
the
 
first
 
3
 
words
 
of
 
your
 
line.
 5.  Multi-Modal  Practice:  You  can  be  in  Ladder  Mode  (adding  lines  one  by  one),  
with
 
Chaos
 
flashing
 
in
 
the
 
background,
 
using
 
Mnemonic
 
(first
 
letter)
 
text,
 
while
 
an
 
Aggressive
 
AI
 
reader
 
is
 
shouting
 
your
 
cues.

hand-off.
 
It
 
is
 
designed
 
to
 
be
 
a
